Title: Historia de Abiponibus, equestri bellicosaque Paraquariae natione, locupletata copiosis barbararum gentium, urbium, fluminum, ferarum, amphibiorum, insectorum, serpentium praecipuorum, piscium, avium, arborum, plantarum aliarumque eiusdem provinciae proprietatum observationibus; pars prima
Creator: Martin Dobrizhoffer, presbyter, for eighteen years a missionary in Paraguay (title page: 'Authore Martino Dobrizhoffer')
Publisher: Vienna: typis Josephi nob. de Kurzbek (title page)
Publication Date: 1784 (title page: 'Anno 1784.', read on the page image, leaf n6, and at OCR line 55)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1784); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: lat
Source: archive.org item historiadeabipon00dobr, full text from the item's own djvu.txt (historiadeabipon00dobr_djvu.txt). Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (980,381) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1784, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fairly legible Latin with the long s read as f and scattered garbles. A John Carter Brown Library stamp stands at OCR line 4. Word count of the host text: 125,048. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The first of three parts; the title page does not number it, and the index is headed 'Index Primae Partis' (OCR line 432). The author's preface to the reader at about 75-430; index of sections at 430-690. 'Liber prodromus de Paraquariae habitu', the introductory book on the state of Paraguay, from 697 to 21249, with no numbered chapters in the body; the thirty Guarani towns at about 1165-1290. Errata from 21260 to the end. Parts 2 and 3, on the Abipones themselves, were not taken.
